### bone marrow of calvaria `http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_1200343`

#### Added
- Class: [bone marrow of calvaria](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_1200343) 

- [bone marrow of calvaria](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_1200343) [IAO_0000233](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/IAO_0000233) "https://github.com/obophenotype/uberon/pull/3758"^^[anyURI](http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#anyURI) 

- [bone marrow of calvaria](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_1200343) [terminology_notes](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBPROP_0000013) "The differentia uses 'vault of skull' (UBERON:0004339), whose own comment records that the vault may not be precisely equivalent to the calvaria - sources differ on whether the temporal, ethmoid and sphenoid bones are included. That ambiguity is inherited here rather than introduced: this class is intended as the marrow of whatever 'vault of skull' covers, which is the compartment the calvarial bone marrow literature samples." 

- [bone marrow of calvaria](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_1200343) [contributor](http://purl.org/dc/terms/contributor) [0000-0002-6601-2165](https://orcid.org/0000-0002-6601-2165) 

- [bone marrow of calvaria](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_1200343) [date](http://purl.org/dc/terms/date) "2026-08-20T04:59:35Z"^^[dateTime](http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#dateTime) 

- [bone marrow of calvaria](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_1200343) [hasExactSynonym](http://www.geneontology.org/formats/oboInOwl#hasExactSynonym) "calvarial bone marrow" 

- [bone marrow of calvaria](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_1200343) [hasExactSynonym](http://www.geneontology.org/formats/oboInOwl#hasExactSynonym) "calvarial marrow" 

- [bone marrow of calvaria](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_1200343) [hasRelatedSynonym](http://www.geneontology.org/formats/oboInOwl#hasRelatedSynonym) "skull cap bone marrow" 

- [bone marrow of calvaria](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_1200343) [id](http://www.geneontology.org/formats/oboInOwl#id) "UBERON:1200343" 

- [bone marrow of calvaria](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_1200343) [external_ontology_notes](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBPROP_0000012) "Neither the FMA nor SNOMED has a calvarial marrow grouping class. The FMA covers the same territory with per-bone classes: FMA:303380 (frontal), FMA:303382 (parietal), FMA:303388 (occipital) and FMA:303390 (sutural bone); these are the natural xref targets if regional calvarial marrow terms are added." 
  - [external_ontology](http://www.geneontology.org/formats/oboInOwl#external_ontology) "FMA" 

- [bone marrow of calvaria](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_1200343) [IAO_0000115](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/IAO_0000115) "A bone marrow of skull that is located within the diploic spaces of the calvaria (skull vault). It is the principal haematopoietic compartment of the mammalian skull and is connected to the dura mater by transcalvarial channels; its immune composition differs between anterior (frontal, parietal) and posterior (interparietal, occipital) territories." 
  - [hasDbXref](http://www.geneontology.org/formats/oboInOwl#hasDbXref) "PMID:35301477" 

  - [hasDbXref](http://www.geneontology.org/formats/oboInOwl#hasDbXref) "PMID:42618784" 

  - [hasDbXref](http://www.geneontology.org/formats/oboInOwl#hasDbXref) "PMID:41389063" 

- [bone marrow of calvaria](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_1200343) [comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) "In the mouse, lymphoid clusters containing follicular helper-like T cells, B cells and follicular dendritic cells are enriched in the marrow of the occipital and interparietal regions and are sparse in the frontal and parietal regions (PMID:42618784)." 

- [bone marrow of calvaria](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_1200343) [label](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#label) "bone marrow of calvaria" 

- [bone marrow of calvaria](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_1200343) EquivalentTo [bone marrow of skull](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_1200342) and ([BFO_0000050](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/BFO_0000050) some [vault of skull](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_0004339)) 

- [bone marrow of calvaria](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_1200343) SubClassOf [bone marrow of skull](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_1200342) 

- [bone marrow of calvaria](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_1200343) SubClassOf [BFO_0000050](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/BFO_0000050) some [vault of skull](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_0004339)

- [bone marrow of skull](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_1200342) [hasRelatedSynonym](http://www.geneontology.org/formats/oboInOwl#hasRelatedSynonym) "cranial bone marrow" 

- [bone marrow of skull](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_1200342) [id](http://www.geneontology.org/formats/oboInOwl#id) "UBERON:1200342" 

- [bone marrow of skull](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_1200342) [external_ontology_notes](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBPROP_0000012) "SCTID:712962003 ('Structure of bone marrow of cranium') is scoped to the cranium rather than the whole skull, so it excludes mandibular marrow that this class admits; its SNOMED children cover frontal, temporal, sphenoid and nasal-orbit marrow. The FMA has no corresponding grouping class - it descends from 'Bone marrow of flat bone' (FMA:303320) straight to per-bone classes such as FMA:303380 (frontal), FMA:303382 (parietal), FMA:303388 (occipital) and FMA:303454 (temporal) - so no FMA xref is asserted here." 
  - [external_ontology](http://www.geneontology.org/formats/oboInOwl#external_ontology) "FMA" 

- [bone marrow of skull](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_1200342) [IAO_0000115](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/IAO_0000115) "A bone marrow that is located within the bones of the skull. In mammals, skull bone marrow is haematopoietically active and communicates directly with the dura mater through osseous skull channels, which permit the exchange of cerebrospinal fluid, central nervous system-derived antigens and immune cells; it acts as a local reservoir of myeloid cells and B cells for the meninges and the central nervous system." 
  - [hasDbXref](http://www.geneontology.org/formats/oboInOwl#hasDbXref) "PMID:34083447" 

  - [hasDbXref](http://www.geneontology.org/formats/oboInOwl#hasDbXref) "PMID:35301477" 

  - [hasDbXref](http://www.geneontology.org/formats/oboInOwl#hasDbXref) "PMID:42618784" 

  - [hasDbXref](http://www.geneontology.org/formats/oboInOwl#hasDbXref) "PMID:30150661" 

  - [hasDbXref](http://www.geneontology.org/formats/oboInOwl#hasDbXref) "PMID:37996526" 

- [bone marrow of skull](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_1200342) [hasRelatedSynonym](http://www.geneontology.org/formats/oboInOwl#hasRelatedSynonym) "skull BM" 
  - [hasSynonymType](http://www.geneontology.org/formats/oboInOwl#hasSynonymType) [OMO_0003000](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/OMO_0003000) 

- [bone marrow of skull](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_1200342) [comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) "Distinguished from other bone marrow sites (for example sternal, femoral, tibial or vertebral marrow) by its direct osseous connection to the dura mater. Reported to contain lymphoid structures with germinal-centre-like features that respond to central nervous system-derived antigens (PMID:42618784)." 

- [bone marrow of skull](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_1200342) [label](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#label) "bone marrow of skull" 

- [bone marrow of skull](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_1200342) EquivalentTo [bone marrow](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_0002371) and ([BFO_0000050](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/BFO_0000050) some [skull](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_0003129)) 

- [bone marrow of skull](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_1200342) SubClassOf [bone marrow](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_0002371) 

- [bone marrow of skull](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_1200342) SubClassOf [BFO_0000050](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/BFO_0000050) some [skull](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_0003129)

- [epithelium of tip of renal papilla](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_8930106) [IAO_0000115](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/IAO_0000115) "An epithelium that is part of the tip of a renal papilla, covering the papillary surface where it projects into the minor calyx and where the papillary collecting ducts open.   - [hasDbXref](http://www.geneontology.org/formats/oboInOwl#hasDbXref) "FMA:280216"

- [inner cortex of lens](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_8930000) [IAO_0000115](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/IAO_0000115) "A zone of the lens cortex that lies immediately external to the lens nucleus and internal to the outer cortex of lens. It is composed of older, fully denucleated lens fibre cells whose lamellae remain largely intact but which are more compacted than those of the outer cortex." 
  - [hasDbXref](http://www.geneontology.org/formats/oboInOwl#hasDbXref) "PMID:20171212" 

  - [hasDbXref](http://www.geneontology.org/formats/oboInOwl#hasDbXref) "PMID:10550792" 

- [inner cortex of lens](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_8930000) [comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) "Equivalent to the 'deep cortex of lens' (cortex profundus) in FMA. The boundary with the lens nucleus is a continuous gradient of fibre-cell age and compaction but is reliably visible as an optical discontinuity on Scheimpflug/slit-lamp imaging." 

- [inner cortex of lens](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_8930000) [label](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#label) "inner cortex of lens"

- [inner periarterial lymphatic sheath of spleen](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_8930193) [IAO_0000115](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/IAO_0000115) "The central region of the periarterial lymphatic sheath of the spleen, immediately surrounding the central arteriole. It forms the thymus-dependent (T cell) area of the splenic white pulp, is largely devoid of reticulum cells, and contains interdigitating cells in close contact with T lymphocytes."

  - [hasDbXref](http://www.geneontology.org/formats/oboInOwl#hasDbXref) "DOI:10.1007/BF00217084"

- [inner periarterial lymphatic sheath of spleen](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_8930193) [comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) "Inner/outer PALS subdivision is described in rat and mouse (Veerman &amp; van Ewijk 1975, doi:10.1007/BF00225103). In adult human spleen the T-cell regions are discontinuous and lack rodent-type PALS architecture (van Krieken et al. 1985, doi:10.1111/j.1365-2559.1985.tb02841.x; Steiniger, R&uuml;ttinger &amp; Barth 2003, doi:10.1177/002215540305100511); no inner/outer subdivision has been demonstrated in humans."

- [outer cortex of lens](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_8930001) [IAO_0000115](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/IAO_0000115) "A zone of the lens cortex that lies immediately beneath the lens capsule and epithelium and surrounds the inner cortex of lens. It is composed of the most recently differentiated lens fibre cells, added from the lens epithelium at the equator; it contains the remodelling and transition zones in which fibre cells lose their nuclei." 
  - [hasDbXref](http://www.geneontology.org/formats/oboInOwl#hasDbXref) "PMID:19357350" 

  - [hasDbXref](http://www.geneontology.org/formats/oboInOwl#hasDbXref) "PMID:10550792" 

- [outer cortex of lens](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_8930001) [comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) "Equivalent to the 'superficial cortex of lens' (cortex superficialis) in FMA. The outer cortex is the site of ongoing lens fibre cell maturation, including the remodelling zone (RZ) and transition zone (TZ) described by Lim et al. 2009 (PMID:19357350)." 

- [outer cortex of lens](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_8930001) [label](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#label) "outer cortex of lens"

  - [hasDbXref](http://www.geneontology.org/formats/oboInOwl#hasDbXref) "DOI:10.1007/BF00217084" 

- [outer periarterial lymphatic sheath of spleen](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_8930194) [IAO_0000115](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/IAO_0000115) "The peripheral region of the periarterial lymphatic sheath of the spleen, surrounding the inner periarterial lymphatic sheath of spleen, in which reticulum cells are arranged in cylindrical shells. It contains both T and B lymphocytes and is a site of T-B cell interaction and of antigen-induced B cell proliferation and early plasmablast development, from where plasmablasts migrate towards the red pulp." 
  - [hasDbXref](http://www.geneontology.org/formats/oboInOwl#hasDbXref) "DOI:10.1007/BF00221456" 

  - [hasDbXref](http://www.geneontology.org/formats/oboInOwl#hasDbXref) "DOI:10.1007/BF00217084" 

  - [hasDbXref](http://www.geneontology.org/formats/oboInOwl#hasDbXref) "DOI:10.1007/BF00225103" 

- [outer periarterial lymphatic sheath of spleen](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_8930194) [hasExactSynonym](http://www.geneontology.org/formats/oboInOwl#hasExactSynonym) "peripheral PALS" 
  - [hasDbXref](http://www.geneontology.org/formats/oboInOwl#hasDbXref) "DOI:10.1007/BF00225103" 

- [outer periarterial lymphatic sheath of spleen](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_8930194) [comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) "Inner/outer PALS subdivision is described in rat and mouse (Veerman &amp; van Ewijk 1975, doi:10.1007/BF00225103). In adult human spleen the T-cell regions are discontinuous and lack rodent-type PALS architecture (van Krieken et al. 1985, doi:10.1111/j.1365-2559.1985.tb02841.x; Steiniger, R&uuml;ttinger &amp; Barth 2003, doi:10.1177/002215540305100511); no inner/outer subdivision has been demonstrated in humans." 

- [outer periarterial lymphatic sheath of spleen](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_8930194) [label](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#label) "outer periarterial lymphatic sheath of spleen" 

- [outer periarterial lymphatic sheath of spleen](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_8930194) SubClassOf [organ part](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_0000064) 

- [outer periarterial lymphatic sheath of spleen](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_8930194) SubClassOf [BFO_0000050](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/BFO_0000050) some [periarterial lymphatic sheath](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_0001960) 

- [outer periarterial lymphatic sheath of spleen](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_8930194) SubClassOf [RO_0002221](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/RO_0002221) some [inner periarterial lymphatic sheath of spleen](http://purl.obolibrary.org/obo/UBERON_8930193)
